Data tertanam (offline instan) + sinkronisasi live opsional ke API publik resmi api.buku.cloudapp.web.id dengan rantai proxy CORS sebagai cadangan. */ function sibiBookUrl(rel){if(!rel)return '';return /^(https?:)?\/\//i.test(rel)?rel:(window.__SIBI_CDN+rel);} function sibiJenjangLabel(l){return {paud:'PAUD',sd:'SD/MI',smp:'SMP/MTs',sma:'SMA/MA',smk:'SMK/MAK',smasmmk:'SMA/MA/SMK/MAK',other:'Lainnya'}[l]||'Lainnya';} function sibiKurLabel(u){return u==='merdeka'?'Kurikulum Merdeka':(u==='k13'?'Kurikulum 2013':'Lainnya');} function sibiKurShort(u){return u==='merdeka'?'Merdeka':(u==='k13'?'K-13':'Lainnya');} function sibiTypeLabel(b){return b==='g'?'Buku Guru':(b==='s'?'Buku Siswa':'Bacaan/Nonteks');} function sibiClassLabel(b){return (b.k&&b.k!=='0')?('Kelas '+b.k):sibiJenjangLabel(b.l);} window.__sibi={all:[],view:[],shown:24,f:{q:'',l:'',k:'',m:'',u:'',b:''},ready:false,searchIdx:null,syncing:false,autoTried:false,fromCache:false,syncAt:''}; window.__sibi.ready=true; } function sibiMapelOptions(){ var set={},arr=[]; window.__sibi.all.forEach(function(b){if(!set[b.m]){set[b.m]=1;arr.push(b.m);}}); return arr.sort(function(a,b){return a.localeCompare(b,'id');}); } function sibiTglID(iso){ try{var d=iso&&iso.length===10?new Date(iso+'T00:00:00'):new Date(); return d.toLocaleDateString('id-ID',{day:'numeric',month:'short',year:'numeric'});}catch(e){return iso||'';} } function sibiOpts(arr,sel,allLabel){ var h='';
